# Agents · W · part 8

1,027 entries, workstation-pitfalls to writer-opus-5-5-medium. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **workstation-pitfalls**: This file is part of the agent working agreement in [AGENTS.md](../../AGENTS.md). It records lessons that were first learned in Claude Code sessions and kept only in that runtime's private memory. They apply equally to Codex, Claude Code, and any other agent working in this clone. Each rule names the failure it prevents. ([fjure/idle-factory-game](https://github.com/fjure/idle-factory-game/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/workstation-pitfalls.md))
- **workstream-coordinator**: Coordinates a single linear branch of research for one approved goal. Produces incremental report updates with claim status, provenance, uncertainty, and escalation points. ([dzackgarza/research](https://github.com/dzackgarza/research/blob/HEAD/archives/provenance/lattice-research/agents/agents/workstream-coordinator.md))
- **workstream-curator**: Decide which newly created digest workstreams are near-duplicates of existing ones, so they can be merged. Judgement only — reads nothing, writes nothing. ([pariidanDKE/convo-digest](https://github.com/pariidanDKE/convo-digest/blob/HEAD/agents/workstream-curator.md))
- **workstream-groomer**: Grooms a workstream's still-open questions and open tasks when new evidence arrives. Use when /pickup, /end-lane or /close-issue dispatches an event groom — even for one row. The full sweep is /groom: a different test. ([radleta/claude-plugins](https://github.com/radleta/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/scratch-memory/agents/workstream-groomer.md))
- **workstream-lead**: Coordinates execution for a specific launch workstream (content, demand, enablement, success). ([gtmagents/gtm-agents](https://github.com/gtmagents/gtm-agents/blob/HEAD/plugins/product-launch-orchestration/agents/workstream-lead.md) · ★447 · 6 copies)
- **workstream-lead**: - **Agent Name**: workstream-lead - **Role**: Workstream management and delivery coordination lead - **Phase**: production ([5throck/ai-workspace-standards](https://github.com/5throck/ai-workspace-standards/blob/HEAD/templates/co-consult/docs/lifecycle/agents/workstream-lead.md) · ★25)
- **workstream-lead**: Workstream lead - manages specific workstreams within consulting projects, coordinates team members, tracks progress, and ensures delivery quality. Use when: managing project workstreams, coordinating team execution, tracking milestones, or managing day-to-day project operations. ([5throck/ai-workspace-standards](https://github.com/5throck/ai-workspace-standards/blob/HEAD/templates/co-consult/agents/workstream-lead.md) · ★25)
- **workstream-planner**: Decomposes a task into candidate workstreams and calls jev_plan_workstreams (Jev System One) to get lane/priority/risk/independence/conflict decisions in one speculative fan-out request. Returns a structured execution plan (parallel_safe vs needs_sequential). Does not edit files — the coordinator executes the plan. ([chrismarchant/jev-router](https://github.com/chrismarchant/jev-router/blob/HEAD/agents/workstream-planner.md))
- **workstream-planner**: Read-only release-planning agent. Takes an unordered backlog of change tasks plus a repository path and returns a safe execution plan — parallel waves of independent tasks separated by verification gates — by deriving write-sets from the code, then building semantic, contention, and sweep edges. Use proactively before implementing 3+ coupled tasks in one repo, or before spawning parallel implementation subagents, so workers never edit the same file. ([omidemadi1/skills](https://github.com/omidemadi1/skills/blob/HEAD/agents/workstream-planner.md))
- **workstream-sentry**: Skannar hela 1753 SKINCARE-projektet efter buggar, UX-brister, säljhinder, designinkonsekvenser och tillgänglighetsproblem. Readonly – rapporterar findings men redigerar aldrig. ([chgenberg/1753](https://github.com/chgenberg/1753/blob/HEAD/agents/workstream-sentry.md))
- **workstream-worker**: Executes one independent workstream by offloading it to a Mistral-hosted model via the mistral_delegate MCP tool, and returns a strict-JSON result for the coordinator to merge. ([chrismarchant/mistral-code-router](https://github.com/chrismarchant/mistral-code-router/blob/HEAD/agents/workstream-worker.md))
- **worktree**: Git worktree lifecycle — create isolated workspaces for parallel development, manage branches, cleanup. Max 3 active worktrees. Called by team for parallel streams. ([Rune-kit/rune](https://github.com/Rune-kit/rune/blob/HEAD/agents/worktree.md) · ★93)
- **worktree**: Git worktree 생성, 목록 조회, 삭제를 관리하는 에이전트. 브랜치명 정규화, tmux 세션 자동 생성, 한글 에러 메시지 지원 ([orientpine/honeypot](https://github.com/orientpine/honeypot/blob/HEAD/plugins/worktree-workflow/agents/worktree.md) · ★37 · 2 copies)
- **WORKTREE**: **Purpose:** How to *work* when this sitting is not on `main` — every edit, every commit, every time a log entry is needed. Wrap/handoff only verify the standing rule was kept. They are not the moment the rule starts. ([jet-flyer/Rocket-Chip](https://github.com/jet-flyer/Rocket-Chip/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/WORKTREE.md) · ★1)
- **worktree**: 本文件拥有 worktree 准备与执行环境的几件互相关联的事：在一个新 worktree 里把工具链准备好、按任务收缩视野、让各 worktree 共用一份本机 HTTPS 证书、让各 worktree 共享同一份构建缓存，以及在未激活 mise 的 shell 里正确执行命令。它们的共同线索是「拿到一个 worktree 之后怎么正确执行命令」，所以放在一起。 ([pan-Z2l0aHVi/mono](https://github.com/pan-Z2l0aHVi/mono/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/worktree.md) · ★1)
- **worktree**: worktree 是另一份 checkout（`.claude/worktrees/&lt;名字&gt;/`）：被追蹤的檔案都在，**`.gitignore` 擋掉的一個都沒有**。 下面這幾樣刻意不進版控，卻是做事要用的。少了的時候有的會大聲壞，有的**安靜地少做一件事** —— 後者最危險（2026-11-05 查的）。 ([b09901017/baobao-wellness-clinic-scheduler](https://github.com/b09901017/baobao-wellness-clinic-scheduler/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/worktree.md))
- **worktree**: Creates the isolated worktree for a /devteam:implement run — runs the repo's worktree skill (or git worktree add when the repo names none) with the capabilities the repo names, runs the repo's setup commands, and checks the services the repo lists. Returns only the path, branch, claimed resources and whether setup passed. ([dgigafox/devteam](https://github.com/dgigafox/devteam/blob/HEAD/agents/worktree.md))
- **worktree**: Interpret, clarify, and confirm worktree add, list, status, and merge requests. ([HellHBBD/dotfiles](https://github.com/HellHBBD/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/opencode/.config/opencode/agents/worktree.md))
- **Worktree Setup**: Use when starting work on any non-trivial task that involves edits, multiple parallel tasks on the same repo, or risk of clobbering another agent/session. Enforces the 'one agent per worktree' rule and produces the wt commands to safely isolate work. ([JaydipGabani/agentkit](https://github.com/JaydipGabani/agentkit/blob/HEAD/agents/worktree-setup.agent.md))
- **worktree-agent**: Use when a subagent should create, manage or clean up git worktrees for parallel work (at most 3 active). Runs the worktree skill's steps. ([linenoize/topia](https://github.com/linenoize/topia/blob/HEAD/agents/worktree-agent.md) · ★1)
- **worktree-agent**: You are a parallel sprint executor in the ADP pipeline. You work in an isolated git worktree on a dedicated branch (`adp/sprint-N`). You implement exactly one task from `tasks.md`, run sensors, and return a result. ([0xPuncker/adp](https://github.com/0xPuncker/adp/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/worktree-agent.md))
- **worktree-agent**: You are a specialized agent for managing git worktrees. Your job is to create, list, and manage worktrees for parallel development. ([launchapp-dev/worktree-manager](https://github.com/launchapp-dev/worktree-manager/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worktree-agent.md))
- **worktree-agent**: Workspace lifecycle agent. Asks the workspace maker (scripts/worktree.py, see docs/how-to/get-a-workspace.md) for the workspace of a feature branch or ticket, and asks the maker to clear a workspace after a branch merges. Getting a workspace is non-destructive (no confirmation required). Clearing one is destructive and requires an explicit "yes" after the safety-check report. Use when: user types /worktree; asks for a workspace for a branch or ticket; asks to clear or close a workspace after a PR merges. ([urlmonitor/leafcutter-ai](https://github.com/urlmonitor/leafcutter-ai/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/worktree-agent.md))
- **worktree-agent-a2b71cf252610c45b** ([prateekjain98/pyper](https://github.com/prateekjain98/pyper/blob/HEAD/coordination/agents/worktree-agent-a2b71cf252610c45b.md))
- **worktree-base-sync**: herdr の Workspace Fork(≒新規 worktree の作成)は親チェックアウトの HEAD を そのまま使い、fetch を挟まない。親を開いたまま何度も fork すると、新しい worktree が origin より何コミットも遅れた base から生まれる。既存の `pr-gate` はこれを SessionStart の advisory(「base 追従: origin/main から N コミット遅れています」)で伝えるが、**ブランチ自体は動かさない** — 履行歴を持つブランチを無断で動かすのは事故なので、これは意図した非対称 だった。 ([tarotene/dotfiles](https://github.com/tarotene/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/worktree-base-sync.md) · ★1)
- **worktree-bootstrap**: Use one worktree per active implementation lane. ([cesarwerlich/multi-agent-scaffold](https://github.com/cesarwerlich/multi-agent-scaffold/blob/HEAD/init/docs/agents/worktree-bootstrap.md))
- **worktree-brief**: Paste this block into the brief of every sub-agent that works in its own worktree (`isolation: "worktree"`). ([fimoklei/maestro](https://github.com/fimoklei/maestro/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/worktree-brief.md) · ★1)
- **worktree-build-prerequisites**: Liza embeds contracts and skills from `internal/embedded/` at build time. In a worktree, those embedded copies can lag behind the repo masters after branch switches or edits. ([liza-mas/liza](https://github.com/liza-mas/liza/blob/HEAD/lessons/agents/worktree-build-prerequisites.md) · ★422)
- **worktree-builder**: Implements ONE scoped ticket in an isolated git worktree and commits to a branch without merging. Use when dispatching parallel builders from orchestrating-parallel-agents — each builder gets one ticket with disjoint file footprint. Not for reviewing code (use branch-reviewer) or for work that must edit the main checkout directly. ([hiteshmodi0624/claude-config](https://github.com/hiteshmodi0624/claude-config/blob/HEAD/payload/agents/worktree-builder.md))
- **worktree-cleaner**: Safely removes git worktrees created for finished tasks. Checks for uncommitted work and merge/PR status before removing, then prunes references and reports what it did. Examples: - "Clean up the worktree for the feature I just merged" - "Remove the worktree for issue 42" ([Calvis-Labs/clanker-starter-kit](https://github.com/Calvis-Labs/clanker-starter-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worktree-cleaner.md))
- **worktree-cleanup**: **Status:** Active reference **Owner:** Barrels Grenada engineering **Last updated:** 2026-11-08 ([Marcus100/grenmet](https://github.com/Marcus100/grenmet/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/worktree-cleanup.md) · ★1)
- **worktree-coder**: Implementation agent for feature work inside a git worktree. You MUST route all coding in a worktree through this agent — it loads the project's language guidelines before writing, then implements against them. ([CrAzyScreamx/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/CrAzyScreamx/claude-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/worktree-guidelines/agents/coder.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **worktree-content-digest**: Use this agent when one git worktree needs a read-only content digest for triage. It reports unique commits, uncommitted substance, and whether untracked files are throwaway or worth keeping in exactly two or three concise lines. ([paulnsorensen/dotfiles](https://github.com/paulnsorensen/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/chezmoi/dot_omp/private_agent/agents/worktree-content-digest.md) · ★2)
- **worktree-coordinator**: Guides agents on using git worktrees for parallel work coordination ([kmesiab/qwenvert](https://github.com/kmesiab/qwenvert/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worktree-coordinator.md) · ★8)
- **worktree-coordinator**: Coordinates worktree creation and registers them in Mem0 for multi-agent tracking ([vanman2024/dev-lifecycle-marketplace](https://github.com/vanman2024/dev-lifecycle-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/supervisor/agents/worktree-coordinator.md) · ★4)
- **worktree-developer**: 並列開発用の非対話型 developer エージェント。プロンプトで指定されたタスクIDのみを実装してコミットして終了する。ユーザーとの Q&A・承認確認は行わない。 ([satoh-y-0323/clade](https://github.com/satoh-y-0323/clade/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worktree-developer.md))
- **worktree-developer**: Non-interactive developer agent for parallel development. Implements only the task IDs specified in the prompt, commits, and exits. Does not perform Q&A or approval confirmation with the user. ([satoh-y-0323/clade](https://github.com/satoh-y-0323/clade/blob/HEAD/templates/en/.claude/agents/worktree-developer.md))
- **worktree-diff-sentinel**: Read-only check that a PlanStepRunner or other multi-worktree run's git diff/status is being reviewed against the real branch, not a stray Worktree/ harness folder. Run before presenting any plan-run review, or whenever a */Worktree/ path shows up in search/diff results. ([sinthor505/RoslynSentinel](https://github.com/sinthor505/RoslynSentinel/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worktree-diff-sentinel.md) · ★1)
- **worktree-discipline**: The main checkout is shared by many agents and the Human. Never work in it directly: no edits, no commits, no stash entries, no experiments, no scratch directories. It stays on `main`, clean, and only fast-forwards. Every task gets its own worktree and branch. ([BotHarness/DeepSeekBot](https://github.com/BotHarness/DeepSeekBot/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/worktree-discipline.md) · ★932)
- **worktree-dispatch**: Isolated mode gives a write-capable routed agent a dedicated Git worktree while leaving the caller's checkout unchanged. It is opt-in and is not a container, sandbox, or credential boundary. ([Buckeyes22/pitwall](https://github.com/Buckeyes22/pitwall/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/worktree-dispatch.md))
- **worktree-doctor**: Diagnose and fix git worktree issues — wrong upstream, detached HEAD, stale worktrees, tracking problems, merge conflicts after rebase. Use when git operations fail in a worktree, when push/pull behaves unexpectedly, or when the user says "worktree is broken" or "git is confused". ([moxer-mmh/claude-toolbox](https://github.com/moxer-mmh/claude-toolbox/blob/HEAD/plugins/worktree-guard/agents/worktree-doctor.md) · ★1)
- **worktree-env**: Copy .env and .env.local from main checkout into a linked git worktree (before execution) ([roborew/opencode-config](https://github.com/roborew/opencode-config/blob/HEAD/agents/worktree-env.md))
- **worktree-file-path-consistency**: Claude Code's Edit tool tracks prior reads by exact string path. If you Read via one path and Edit via a slightly different string (even if both resolve to the same inode), the edit is rejected. ([liza-mas/liza](https://github.com/liza-mas/liza/blob/HEAD/lessons/agents/worktree-file-path-consistency.md) · ★422 · 2 copies)
- **worktree-flow**: One worktree per unit of work, landed by a squash-merged PR, never a direct push to `trunk`. Several Claude Code sessions work against this repo at once, and a worktree gives each one its own working tree, index and `HEAD`. Why each step is shaped the way it is, what gets provisioned, what enforces it and how cleanup works: [`worktree-flow-reference.md`](worktree-flow-reference.md). ([markgoho/doula-cloud](https://github.com/markgoho/doula-cloud/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/worktree-flow.md))
- **worktree-flow-reference**: The rationale, enforcement and history behind the procedure in [`worktree-flow.md`](worktree-flow.md). Read a section here when a step there surprises you, or before you change a hook it names. ([markgoho/doula-cloud](https://github.com/markgoho/doula-cloud/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/worktree-flow-reference.md))
- **worktree-implementer**: Implements ONE backlog issue end-to-end in an isolated git worktree and lands it as an open PR (never merged, never deployed). Use for the standing multi-agent fan-out over model:sonnet / model:opus backlog stories. The worktree-discipline incident classes (case-twin pollution, deploy-from-worktree 0-diff, push-before-merge, doc-sync literal drift) are baked in below — the driver brief only needs the issue number and any issue-specific context. ([averagejoematt/life-platform](https://github.com/averagejoematt/life-platform/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worktree-implementer.md) · ★2)
- **worktree-implementer**: Senior backend systems engineer powered by Gemini 2.5 Flash (flash). - **worktree-implementer**: Implementation agent for isolated worktree tasks with explicit acceptance criteria. ([az9713/agent-balance-kit](https://github.com/az9713/agent-balance-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worktree-implementer.md))
- **worktree-implementer**: Implements one well-specified ParaTrack task in its own git worktree, commits it, and reports. Use for parallel feature work dispatched from the main session. ([Cypher0924/paratrack](https://github.com/Cypher0924/paratrack/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worktree-implementer.md))
- **worktree-implementer**: Delegate when you want isolated git-worktree work that never touches the current branch or working tree: build a self-contained coding task (one feature or fix, or several in parallel), OR check out an existing PR's branch in a worktree to revise it, resolve its merge conflicts, or fix its failing CI — updating that PR without opening a new one. Creates/uses its own worktree, runs the project's own checks, commits, pushes, opens a PR only when building new, then removes the worktree. Works in any git repo and any stack. ([ErikdeJager/ReCue](https://github.com/ErikdeJager/ReCue/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worktree-implementer.md))
- **worktree-implementer**: Implements one self-contained change in its own git worktree, so several can run at once without colliding. Use for the pieces of a fan-out where each piece owns a distinct set of files and can be verified on its own. ([jonjaques/inertialref](https://github.com/jonjaques/inertialref/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worktree-implementer.md))
- **worktree-implementer**: Default worker for one lane of a parallel wave. Use when dispatching a single, well-scoped implementation or fix task that should land as a commit on its own branch, in its own worktree, without merging or touching the tracker. ([Raaif-Yousuf/agent-orchestration](https://github.com/Raaif-Yousuf/agent-orchestration/blob/HEAD/agents/worktree-implementer.md))
- **worktree-integrator-doctor**: worktree-integrator 環境の読み取り専用診断エージェント。バイナリ・設定ファイル （config.toml）・リポジトリ探索・自己診断（doctor）・サーバー状態・MCP 登録を一括で 調査し、原因と対処コマンドを報告する。「server_* が効かない」「設定が読まれていない 気がする」「セットアップ済みなのに動かない」といった worktree-integrator の不調の 一次調査に使う。何も変更しない（修復は呼び出し元が行う）。 ([vimrak-hal/worktree-integrator](https://github.com/vimrak-hal/worktree-integrator/blob/HEAD/plugins/worktree-integrator/agents/worktree-integrator-doctor.md))
- **worktree-integrator-hooks**: worktree-integrator のライフサイクルフック（config.toml の [[hooks.before]] / [[hooks.after_worktree]] / [[hooks.after]]）を設計・記述・検証する専門エージェント。 「worktree 作成後に依存をインストールしたい」「作成完了を通知したい」「Jira や cmux と連携したい」「hooks を追加・編集したい」「hooks がパースエラーになる」 といった依頼で使う。既存の設定を壊さずマージし、書き換えのたびに `worktree-integrator config check` で検証する。hooks 関連以外の設定 （servers / copy / base 等）には触れない。 ([vimrak-hal/worktree-integrator](https://github.com/vimrak-hal/worktree-integrator/blob/HEAD/plugins/worktree-integrator/agents/worktree-integrator-hooks.md))

- **worktree-isolation**: Worktree isolation is a requirement of the system, not a preference. Every other [flow](../guide/glossary.md#flow) is written to follow it, and a procedure that breaks it has a defect. - **worktree-janitor**: Reports which Ritemark Git worktrees could be reclaimed and why the rest are held back. Never deletes anything: removal is a separate, human-authorized step. - **worktree-janitor**: Proactively audits .worktrees/* against merged/closed branches and uncommitted state. Reports removal candidates — never deletes. ([sh-ai-x/dev-harness-kit](https://github.com/sh-ai-x/dev-harness-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/worktree-janitor.md) · ★1)
- **worktree-janitor**: マージ済み (または独自 commit の無い) 子タスク 1 本ぶんの worktree と local branch を片付ける read-only 寄りの掃除エージェント。worktree が既に消えて branch だけ残った場合も片付ける。親 (監督役) が子の archive 後に background で起動する。確認をすべて満たしたときだけ worktree remove → branch -D/-d → worktree prune を実行し、満たさなければ何もせず理由を返す。PR 作成・マージ・archive・remote branch 削除はしない。 ([ippoan/claude-skills](https://github.com/ippoan/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worktree-janitor.md))
- **worktree-janitor**: A janitor who cannot stand a messy jj @. Shapes the given scope into a clean, described stack and reports it. Spawned by /worktree-janitor, which audits the result; call that skill rather than this agent. ([luizhcrocha/skills](https://github.com/luizhcrocha/skills/blob/HEAD/agents/worktree-janitor.md))
- **worktree-keeper**: Narrow specialist in worktree and branch discipline — the `omp worktree` verbs, `tower/&lt;fleet&gt;/&lt;id&gt;` branch naming, isolated lane worktrees, path-scoped commits and integration, and settle/retire cleanup. ([VeigaPunk/ompskills](https://github.com/VeigaPunk/ompskills/blob/HEAD/archive/shokunin-2026-10-09/agents/worktree-keeper.md))
- **worktree-lead**: Manages implementation of a single component in an isolated git worktree. Coordinates TDD implementation using test-builder and coder sub agents. - **worktrees**: - **Environment**: a worktree runs its tests on `.env.example` defaults (README, "Running it", has the `pnpm test` line). The main checkout's `.env` holds the box's secrets and stays in the main checkout. - **`/implement`** is human-invoked only: paste its steps into a worktree agent's prompt. - **Merging**: tickets touching `import/` (`fetch.py`, `main.py`) conflict with each other. Cherry-pick e ([gantover/iceskatesong.com](https://github.com/gantover/iceskatesong.com/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/worktrees.md))

Остальные документы ссылаются сюда, а не пересказывают. Постановка — [`../tasks/WORKTREE-1-agent-worktrees.md`](../tasks/WORKTREE-1-agent-worktrees.md). - **worktrees**: One writer per working tree. Agents land work from their own worktree; the canonical checkout belongs to whoever is sitting in it. - **wr-newsletter-editor**: Editorial-sim reviewer for AI-generated newsletter drafts. - **wr-newsletter-shape**: Cross-edition shape gate for AI Engineering newsletter artefacts (The Shift, Tokens Spent). - **wr-newsletter-skeptic**: Adversarial reader for AI-generated newsletter artifacts (The Shift, Tokens Spent). Returns a SKEPTIC_REVIEW block with a mechanical SKEPTIC_VERDICT. Runs in fresh context to break the drafter's confirmation bias. Target-agnostic: reviews a brief body… ([windyroad/windyroad](https://github.com/windyroad/windyroad/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wr-newsletter-skeptic.md))

- **writer-method-cluster**: Method writing cluster for technical methodology sections. Use --task parameter: overview (system architecture), algorithm (detailed algorithms), math (mathematical modeling), implementation (technical details), complexity (performance analysis). Examples:\n- &lt;example&gt;\n Context: User needs system architecture description.\n user: "/agent method-cluster --task overview: Describe multimodal learning architecture"\n assistant: "I'll use method-cluster with overview task to create comprehensive system architecture description."\n &lt;commentary&gt;\n System overview needed, use method-cluster with --t… ([liq22/Claude_Code_for_Research_Config](https://github.com/liq22/Claude_Code_for_Research_Config/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/writer-method-cluster.md) · ★1)
- **writer-monthly**: Stage 2 da pipeline mensal — recebe `prioritized.md` aprovado pelo editor + `_internal/raw-destaques.json`, escreve a edição mensal completa em `data/monthly/{YYMM}/draft.md` seguindo `context/templates/newsletter-monthly.md`. Cada destaque é narrativa multi-artigo cobrindo um tema do mês. Gera 3 opções de subject line auto-derivadas.
